### [1] [DAXFS: A Lock-Free Shared Filesystem for CXL Disaggregated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01620)
*Cong Wang,Yiwei Yang,Yusheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: DaxFS是首个利用CXL共享内存实现无锁多主机协调的Linux文件系统，通过cmpxchg原子操作作为唯一协调原语，实现了无中心化协调器的高并发写入和去中心化页面缓存淘汰，在单主机性能上超越tmpfs，并支持GPU线程扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXL技术使多主机能够共享具有硬件缓存一致性的字节寻址内存，但现有文件系统尚未利用这一特性进行无锁多主机协调。传统分布式文件系统依赖集中式协调器或分布式锁，存在性能瓶颈和可扩展性问题，而CXL提供的跨主机原子操作能力为设计完全去中心化的协调机制提供了新机遇。

Method: DaxFS采用cmpxchg原子操作作为唯一的协调原语。核心技术包括：(1)基于CAS的哈希覆盖层，支持多主机无锁并发写入；(2)协作式共享页缓存，配合新颖的多主机时钟淘汰算法(MH-clock)，通过cmpxchg实现完全去中心化的受害者页面选择。系统在QEMU模拟的CXL 3.0环境中验证，使用TCP转发的原子操作实现跨虚拟主机的共享内存。

Result: 在跨主机竞争场景下，DaxFS保持>99%的CAS准确率，无更新丢失。在单主机DRAM支持的DAX上，DaxFS在所有写工作负载中吞吐量均超过tmpfs：4线程随机写吞吐量提升高达2.68倍，64KB随机读吞吐量提升1.18倍。GPU微基准测试表明，基于cmpxchg的设计可扩展至GPU线程，在PCIe 5.0带宽限制下执行页缓存操作。

Conclusion: DaxFS成功证明了利用CXL共享内存和cmpxchg原子操作实现无锁多主机文件系统协调的可行性。该设计消除了对集中式协调器的依赖，实现了高效的去中心化协调，在单主机和多主机场景下均展现出优异性能，并具备向GPU计算的扩展能力，为CXL环境下的文件系统设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: CXL (Compute Express Link) enables multiple hosts to share byte-addressable memory with hardware cache coherence, but no existing filesystem exploits this for lock-free multi-host coordination. We present DaxFS, a Linux filesystem for CXL shared memory that uses cmpxchg atomic operations, which CXL makes coherent across host boundaries, as its sole coordination primitive. A CAS-based hash overlay enables lock-free concurrent writes from multiple hosts without any centralized coordinator. A cooperative shared page cache with a novel multi-host clock eviction algorithm (MH-clock) provides demand-paged caching in shared DAX memory, with fully decentralized victim selection via cmpxchg. We validate multi-host correctness using QEMU-emulated CXL 3.0, where two virtual hosts share a memory region with TCP-forwarded atomics. Under cross-host contention, DaxFS maintains >99% CAS accuracy with no lost updates. On single-host DRAM-backed DAX, DaxFS exceeds tmpfs throughput across all write workloads, achieving up to 2.68x higher random write throughput with 4 threads and 1.18x higher random read throughput at 64 KB. Preliminary GPU microbenchmarks show that the cmpxchg-based design extends to GPU threads performing page cache operations at PCIe 5.0 bandwidth limits.

</details>


### [2] [HACache: Leveraging Read Performance with Cache in a Heterogeneous Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01655)
*Jialin Liu,Liang Shi,Dingcui Yu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出HACache，一种面向异构RAID阵列的异构自适应缓存机制，通过将高性能SSD作为读缓存来重新平衡请求分布，解决了传统条带化机制在异构SSD阵列中的性能瓶颈问题，带宽提升约35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在成本敏感的部署场景中，RAID阵列可能由不同性能等级的SSD组成（如老化降级但仍可用的SSD，或故障后替换为性能更好的新设备）。虽然这降低了采购成本，但带来了性能挑战：传统条带化机制均匀分配请求，较慢的SSD成为瓶颈，导致较快SSD利用率不足，整体带宽受限于最慢的驱动器。

Method: 提出HACache（异构自适应缓存）：1) 形式化请求分流问题并进行形式化求解；2) 采用两阶段请求分流比例调整机制，支持运行时最优分流比例搜索；3) 引入缓存容量调节机制，根据命中率和请求分流需求为每个后端SSD自适应调整配额，从而最大化带宽利用率。

Result: 实验结果表明，HACache显著提升了异构RAID的读性能，在典型的混合配置中带宽提升约35%。

Conclusion: HACache通过引入高性能SSD作为读缓存并采用请求分流与缓存容量调节机制，有效解决了异构SSD阵列的性能不平衡问题，为成本敏感场景下的存储系统优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In cost-sensitive deployments, RAID arrays may combine SSDs with different performance levels. Such heterogeneity arises when aging SSDs degrade yet remain usable, or when failed drives are replaced with new devices of explicitly better performance. While this reduces procurement cost, it creates performance challenges: traditional striping mecahnism distributes requests evenly, but slower SSDs become bottlenecks, leaving faster ones underutilized and limiting overall bandwidth to the slowest drive.
  To address this, we propose HACache (Heterogeneity Adaptive Cache) for read-intensive workloads. HACache introduces high-performance SSDs as read caches to rebalance request distribution. First, we formalize the request diversion problem and solve it formally. Second, to support optimal diversion ratios searching at runtime, HACache adopts a two-phase request diversion ratio adjustment mechanism. Finally, a cache capacity regulation is adopted to adapt quotas for each backend SSD based on hit rates and request diversion needs. This design maximizes bandwidth utilization. Experiments show HACache improves heterogeneous RAID read performance significantly, with bandwidth gains of about 35\% in typical mixed configurations.

</details>

### [3] [FlatAttention: Dataflow and Fabric Collectives Co-Optimization for Large Attention-Based Model Inference on Tile-Based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02110)
*Chi Zhang,Luca Colagrande,Renzo Andri,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FlatAttention数据流，面向MoE模型的tile-based加速器优化注意力计算，相比FlashAttention-3实现4.1x加速和16x HBM流量降低，在端到端DeepSeek-v3解码中实现1.9x系统吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型推理中注意力计算占据主导地位，现有tile-based加速器的片上互连和HBM访问效率亟待优化，需高效数据流管理以充分利用硬件资源。

Method: 利用片上网络集成的集体原语最小化HBM访问，设计适配现代注意力变体的数据流，在32x32 tile配置上实现高硬件利用率。

Result: 在类GH200峰值性能配置上实现最高92.3%利用率，平均compute-bound注意力86%利用率，memory-bound注意力78% HBM带宽利用率；端到端DeepSeek-v3 FP8解码在wafer-scale系统上实现1.9x系统吞吐提升和1.4x延迟降低。

Conclusion: FlatAttention在tile-based加速器上高效支持多种注意力变体，显著优于现有方案，证明了数据流优化对MoE推理加速的关键作用。

Abstract: Attention accounts for an increasingly dominant fraction of total computation during inference for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, making efficient acceleration critical. Emerging domain-specific accelerators for large model inference are shifting toward chip-scale and wafer-scale tile-based architectures. Tiles contain large matrix and vector engines and are connected through on-chip interconnects, which support tile-to-tile traffic to reduce the tile-to-main-memory traffic bottleneck. Hence, dataflow management is crucial to achieve high utilization. We propose FlatAttention, a dataflow for modern attention variants on tile-based accelerators. FlatAttention minimizes expensive high-bandwidth memory (HBM) accesses by exploiting collective primitives integrated into the on-chip network fabric, achieving up to 92.3% utilization, 4.1x speedup over FlashAttention-3, and 16x lower HBM traffic. On a 32x32 tile configuration with peak performance comparable to NVIDIA GH200, FlatAttention generalizes across multiple attention variants, achieving an average of 86% utilization for compute-bound attentions and 78% HBM bandwidth utilization for memory-bound ones, resulting in an average 1.9x speedup over attention implementations on GH200. Finally, we evaluate end-to-end DeepSeek-v3 FP8 decoding with FlatAttention on a wafer-scale multi-die system, achieving a 1.9x improvement in system throughput and a 1.4x reduction in per-user token output latency, despite operating with 1.5x lower peak system performance compared to the state-of-the-art solution.

</details>


### [4] [GEMM-GS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting on Tensor Cores with GEMM-Compatible Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02120)
*Haomin Li,Bowen Zhu,Fangxin Liu,Zongwu Wang,Xinran Liang,Li Jiang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出GEMM-GS，通过将3DGS的混合过程重构为GEMM兼容形式以利用GPU Tensor Cores，实现了1.42倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF和3DGS在3D场景重建中存在渲染延迟问题，现有加速工作忽视了现代GPU Tensor Cores的潜力，因为3DGS流水线缺乏GEMM操作。

Method: 提出GEMM-GS方法，将3DGS混合过程等价重构为GEMM兼容形式以利用Tensor Cores；设计高性能CUDA内核，采用三阶段双缓冲流水线重叠计算与内存访问。

Result: GEMM-GS相比原始3DGS实现1.42倍加速，与现有加速方法结合时平均额外提供1.47倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过GEMM友好的变换有效利用Tensor Cores，为3DGS提供了显著的性能提升，且可与现有方法互补。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) enables 3D scene reconstruction from several 2D images but incurs high rendering latency via its point-sampling design. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) improves on NeRF with explicit scene representation and an optimized pipeline yet still fails to meet practical real-time demands. Existing acceleration works overlook the evolving Tensor Cores of modern GPUs because 3DGS pipeline lacks General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) operations. This paper proposes GEMM-GS, an acceleration approach utilizing tensor cores on GPUs via GEMM-friendly blending transformation. It equivalently reformulates the 3DGS blending process into a GEMM-compatible form to utilize Tensor Cores. A high-performance CUDA kernel is designed, integrating a three-stage double-buffered pipeline that overlaps computation and memory access. Extensive experiments show that GEMM-GS achieves $1.42\times$ speedup over vanilla 3DGS and provides an additional $1.47\times$ speedup on average when combining with existing acceleration approaches. Code is released at https://github.com/shieldforever/GEMM-GS.

</details>


### [5] [TensorPool: A 3D-Stacked 8.4TFLOPS/4.3W Many-Core Domain-Specific Processor for AI-Native Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02291)
*Marco Bertuletti,Yichao Zhang,Diyou Shen,Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli,Frank K. Gürkaynak,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TensorPool，一种面向6G AI-Native物理层的256核RISC-V可编程基带处理器集群，通过集成张量加速引擎，在满足实时性约束的前提下显著提升了计算吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G无线接入网物理层即将引入AI技术以提升服务质量，但深度优化的AI原生物理层模型计算复杂度远高于传统基带，且在太赫兹载波和基站密集化背景下，基站功耗受限于100W以内，亟需在保证实时性（亚毫秒级）和高吞吐量的同时实现高能效、可编程的解决方案。

Method: 采用领域专用化策略，设计了TensorPool——一个包含256个RISC-V32IMAF可编程核心的集群，加速单元为16个256 MACs/周期（FP16）的张量引擎，并配置4MiB L1便签式存储以实现最大数据复用。采用TSMC N7工艺实现，并探索了3D堆叠技术优化张量引擎与L1存储之间的布线。

Result: 在AI-RAN张量运算中，TensorPool实现3643 MACs/周期（张量单元利用率89%），相比无张量加速的核心集群吞吐量提升6倍，GOPS/W/mm²效率提升9.1倍。3D堆叠实现相比2D方案面积减少2.32倍且频率无损失。

Conclusion: TensorPool证明了通过领域专用化的众核可编程基带处理器集成张量加速引擎，能够有效满足6G AI-Native物理层对高吞吐量、高能效和实时性的严苛需求，为AI在物理层的部署提供了可行的硬件基础。

Abstract: The upcoming integration of AI in the physical layer (PHY) of 6G radio access networks (RAN) will enable a higher quality of service in challenging transmission scenarios. However, deeply optimized AI-Native PHY models impose higher computational complexity compared to conventional baseband, challenging deployment under the sub-msec real-time constraints typical of modern PHYs. Additionally, following the extension to terahertz carriers, the upcoming densification of 6G cell-sites further limits the power consumption of base stations, constraining the budget available for compute ($\leq$ 100W). The desired flexibility to ensure long term sustainability and the imperative energy-efficiency gains on the high-throughput tensor computations dominating AI-Native PHYs can be achieved by domain-specialization of many-core programmable baseband processors. Following the domain-specialization strategy, we present TensorPool, a cluster of 256 RISCV32IMAF programmable cores, accelerated by 16 256 MACs/cycle (FP16) tensor engines with low-latency access to 4MiB of L1 scratchpad for maximal data-reuse. Implemented in TSMC's N7, TensorPool achieves 3643~MACs/cycle (89% tensor-unit utilization) on tensor operations for AI-RAN, 6$\times$ more than a core-only cluster without tensor acceleration, while simultaneously improving GOPS/W/mm$^2$ efficiency by 9.1$\times$. Further, we show that 3D-stacking the computing blocks of TensorPool to better unfold the tensor engines to L1-memory routing provides 2.32$\times$ footprint improvement with no frequency degradation, compared to a 2D implementation.

</details>

### [6] [EXaCTz: Guaranteed Extremum Graph and Contour Tree Preservation for Distributed- and GPU-Parallel Lossy Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01397)
*Yuxiao Li,Mingze Xia,Xin Liang,Bei Wang,Hanqi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EXaCTz，一种并行算法，能够在有损压缩的标量场数据中同时保留极值图和等值线树，实现了高达4.52 GB/s的单GPU吞吐量，相比现有方法性能提升最高达3,285倍，并在分布式环境下可扩展至128个GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑保持方法存在三大瓶颈：(1)拓扑校正速度（MB/s级）与压缩速度（GB/s级）存在数量级的吞吐量差距；(2)对多样化拓扑描述符的支持有限；(3)缺乏理论收敛界限。这些问题限制了拓扑保持方法在大规模科学模拟和工作流中的实际应用。

Method: EXaCTz提出了一种高性能、有界迭代的算法，通过为解压数据推导针对性编辑来强制拓扑一致性。与依赖显式拓扑重建的先前方法不同，该算法强制所有顶点的一致最小/最大邻居关系，以及临界点之间的全局排序，从而保证一致的临界点分类、鞍点-极值连接性以及合并/分裂事件的保留。算法的收敛性由脆弱性图中的最长路径界定，该图刻画了校正过程中潜在的级联效应。

Result: 实验结果表明，EXaCTz单GPU吞吐量达4.52 GB/s，相比最先进的等值线树保持方法（Gorski et al.）性能提升高达213倍（单核CPU公平对比）至3,285倍（单GPU版本对比）。在分布式环境中，EXaCTz可扩展至128个GPU，效率达55.6%（对比朴素并行化的6.4%），能在48秒内处理高达512 GB的数据集，实现高达32.69 GB/s的聚合校正吞吐量。

Conclusion: EXaCTz成功解决了现有拓扑保持方法的性能瓶颈，首次实现了与压缩速度相匹配的拓扑校正吞吐量（GB/s级），同时提供了理论收敛保证，并支持极值图和等值线树两种拓扑描述符的保持，为大规模科学数据的拓扑一致性处理提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces EXaCTz, a parallel algorithm that concurrently preserves extremum graphs and contour trees in lossy-compressed scalar field data. While error-bounded lossy compression is essential for large-scale scientific simulations and workflows, existing topology-preserving methods suffer from (1) a significant throughput disparity, where topology correction speeds are on the order of MB/s, lagging orders of magnitude behind compression speeds on the order of GB/s, (2) limited support for diverse topological descriptors, and (3) a lack of theoretical convergence bounds. To address these challenges, EXaCTz introduces a high-performance, bounded-iteration algorithm that enforces topological consistency by deriving targeted edits for decompressed data. Unlike prior methods that rely on explicit topology reconstruction, EXaCTz enforces consistent min/max neighbors of all vertices, along with global ordering among critical points. As such, the algorithm enforces consistent critical-point classification, saddle extremum connectivity, and the preservation of merge/split events. We theoretically prove the convergence of our algorithm, bounded by the longest path in a vulnerability graph that characterizes potential cascading effects during correction. Experiments on real-world datasets show that EXaCTz achieves a single-GPU throughput of up to 4.52 GB/s, outperforming the state-of-the-art contour-tree-preserving method (Gorski et al.) by up to 213x (with a single-core CPU implementation for fair comparison) and 3,285x (with a single-GPU version). In distributed environments, EXaCTz scales to 128 GPUs with 55.6\% efficiency (compared with 6.4\% for a naive parallelization), processing datasets of up to 512 GB in under 48 seconds and achieving an aggregate correction throughput of up to 32.69 GB/s.

</details>


### [7] [ModTrans: Translating Real-world Models for Distributed Training Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01607)
*Yi Lyu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ModTrans，一个将真实世界模型转换为ASTRA-sim分布式训练模拟器输入格式的翻译器，解决了现有模拟器不支持导入实际模型的限制，促进了ML研究者与系统研究者之间的协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式训练是当前机器学习系统的热点研究问题。然而，在没有物理设备和相应资源的情况下进行实验存在困难。虽然可以利用分布式训练模拟器来解决，但现有的模拟器（如ASTRA-sim）不支持导入真实世界开发的模型，这阻碍了机器学习研究者对模拟器的使用。

Method: 开发了ModTrans翻译器，支持将任意真实世界模型转换为ASTRA-sim模拟器所需的输入格式，消除了机器学习专家与机器学习系统研究者之间的障碍。

Result: 实验结果表明ModTrans的开销可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: ModTrans成功实现了真实世界模型与分布式训练模拟器之间的桥接，其低开销特性使其能够成为连接ML研究者与系统研究者的有效工具，推动了分布式训练模拟研究的发展。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed training has been a research hot spot in machine learning systems for industry and academia in recent years. However, conducting experiments without physical machines and corresponding resources is difficult. One solution is to leverage distributed training simulators, but current ones like ASTRA-sim do not support importing real-world developed models, which poses challenges for ML researchers seeking to use them. Based on this challenge, we developed ModTrans, a translator supporting format translation from any real-world model to the ASTRA-sim simulator's input, removing the barrier between machine learning experts and machine learning system researchers. The experiment results show that ModTrans's cost is negligible.

</details>


### [8] [DWDP: Distributed Weight Data Parallelism for High-Performance LLM Inference on NVL72](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01621)
*Wanqian Li,Jintao Peng,Zongfei Jing,Tianyu Zhang,Ze Long,Xianjie Qiao,Xiaoming Chen,Dongxu Yang,Kefeng Duan,June Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DWDP策略，通过按需获取专家权重消除多GPU推理中的同步依赖，在DeepSeek-R1上实现8.8%的TPS/GPU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理并行化策略需要逐层跨rank同步，导致端到端性能对负载不平衡高度敏感，限制了多GPU执行效率。

Method: 提出分布式权重数据并行（DWDP），在保持数据并行执行的同时将MoE权重卸载到对等GPU，按需获取缺失专家；引入分裂权重管理和异步远程权重预取两项优化降低实际开销。

Result: 在TensorRT-LLM中实现DWDP，基于GB200 NVL72平台使用DeepSeek-R1评估，在8K输入/1K输出序列长度、20-100 TPS/user服务范围内，在TPS/user相当的情况下端到端输出TPS/GPU提升8.8%。

Conclusion: DWDP通过消除集体跨rank同步使各GPU独立推进，有效解决了负载不平衡问题，为大规模LLM推理提供了更高效的并行化方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference increasingly depends on multi-GPU execution, yet existing inference parallelization strategies require layer-wise inter-rank synchronization, making end-to-end performance sensitive to workload imbalance. We present DWDP (Distributed Weight Data Parallelism), an inference parallelization strategy that preserves data-parallel execution while offloading MoE weights across peer GPUs and fetching missing experts on demand. By removing collective inter-rank synchronization, DWDP allows each GPU to progress independently. We further address the practical overheads of this design with two optimizations for split-weight management and asynchronous remote-weight prefetch. Implemented in TensorRT-LLM and evaluated with DeepSeek-R1 on GB200 NVL72, DWDP improves end-to-end output TPS/GPU by 8.8% at comparable TPS/user in the 20-100 TPS/user serving range under 8K input sequence length and 1K output sequence length.

</details>


### [9] [Optimization Opportunities for Cloud-Based Data Pipeline Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01954)
*Johannes Jablonski,Georg-Daniel Schwarz,Philip Heltweg,Dirk Riehle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过对现有文献的系统性综述，提出了云计算基础设施中数据管道优化的理论框架，并指出了当前研究在多租户环境和工业评估方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云基础设施需要支持数据管道在成本、速度和资源利用率等方面的高效运行，但目前缺乏对优化机会的综合性视图，因此有必要对现有优化方法进行系统性梳理。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述（Systematic Literature Review）的方法，对关于云基础设施性能优化以支持数据管道的现有研究进行分析与归纳。

Result: 建立了一个包含优化目标（如最小化成本、减少执行时间、成本与完工时间的权衡）及其维度（如单云与多云、批处理与流处理等）的理论框架；同时识别出当前研究的主要空白，包括对多租户环境的探索不足以及缺乏工业界的评估验证。

Conclusion: 本研究为云数据管道优化提供了统一的理论视角，明确了现有研究的局限性，并为未来研究方向提供了建议。

Abstract: Cloud infrastructure supports the efficient operation of data pipelines regarding requirements like cost, speed, and resource utilization. We present an integrated view of optimization opportunities for cloud-based data pipelines by conducting a systematic review of existing literature on optimization approaches to cloud infrastructure performance for data pipelines. Our study contributes a theory of optimization goals like minimizing cost, reducing execution time, and cost-makespan trade-offs, consisting of dimensions such as single vs. multi-cloud, batch vs. stream processing, etc. We highlight gaps in primary research, including the underexploration of multi-tenant environments and lack of industry evaluation, and suggest directions for future research.

</details>

*Heet Nagoriya,Komal Rohit*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云环境中的动态负载变化常导致过度配置从而增加成本。现有的机器学习方法（如LSTM）虽能高效预测但在流量突增时存在延迟；而博弈论等数学启发式方法决策迅速却无法应对未来的负载变化。为了解决这一性能与成本之间的权衡问题，该研究应运而生。

Method: 提出了一种混合编排框架，将基于LSTM的预测性扩展机制与启发式任务分配策略相结合，利用两者优势互补。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在基础设施成本降低方面接近机器学习模型，同时在响应速度上保持了与启发式方法相当的水平。

Conclusion: 该研究为云资源管理提供了一种切实可行的方案，有效提升了成本效率，平衡了预测准确性与调度速度。

Abstract: Cloud computing allows scalable resource provisioning, but dynamic workload changes often lead to higher costs due to over-provisioning. Machine learning (ML) approaches, such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, are effective for predicting workload patterns at a higher level, but they can introduce delays during sudden traffic spikes. In contrast, mathematical heuristics like Game Theory provide fast and reliable scheduling decisions, but they do not account for future workload changes. To address this trade-off, this paper proposes a hybrid orchestration framework that combines LSTM-based predictive scaling with heuristic task allocation. The results show that this approach reduces infrastructure costs close to ML-based models while maintaining fast response times similar to heuristic methods. This work presents a practical approach for improving cost efficiency in cloud resource management.

</details>


### [11] [A Practical Two-Stage Framework for GPU Resource and Power Prediction in Heterogeneous HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02158)
*Beste Oztop,Dhruva Kulkarni,Zhengji Zhao,Ayse Kivilcim Coskun,Kadidia Konate*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析VASP应用的GPU资源与功耗数据，提出一个两阶段资源预测框架，仅用Slurm日志即可实现最高97%的GPU利用率预测准确率，结合DCGM指标后功耗预测准确率达92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算（HPC）对GPU需求的增长，GPU资源与功耗的高效利用变得至关重要。研究旨在通过分析广泛使用的材料科学应用VASP在Perlmutter超算上的历史日志与性能指标，理解其资源利用模式，进而支持更高效的调度决策与功耗感知系统运行。

Method: 研究收集了Slurm工作负载管理器的历史日志与NVIDIA DCGM采集的GPU性能指标。在此基础上，提出了一个两阶段资源预测框架：第一阶段仅使用Slurm日志作为训练数据；第二阶段将DCGM采集的历史GPU性能指标（包括计算与内存活动指标）作为增强特征纳入训练。利用特征工程与机器学习方法，预测平均GPU功耗、最大GPU利用率及最大GPU内存利用率。

Result: 仅使用Slurm提交特征预测最大GPU利用率，准确率高达97%。通过特征工程从GPU计算与内存活动指标中提取的特征与平均功耗表现出良好相关性，运行时功耗预测实验的准确率高达92%。结果表明DCGM指标能有效捕捉应用特征。

Conclusion: DCGM指标能够有效表征应用程序特性，对于开发支持HPC系统动态功耗管理的预测模型具有重要潜力。所提出的框架能够有效预测GPU资源使用与功耗，为实现更智能的资源调度与功耗感知系统操作提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Efficient utilization of GPU resources and power has become critical with the growing demand for GPUs in high-performance computing (HPC). In this paper, we analyze GPU utilization and GPU memory utilization, as well as the power consumption of the Vienna ab initio Simulation Package (VASP), using the Slurm workload manager historical logs and GPU performance metrics collected by NVIDIA's Data Center GPU Manager (DCGM). VASP is a widely used materials science application on Perlmutter at NERSC, an HPE Cray EX system based on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Using our insights from the resource utilization analysis of VASP applications, we propose a resource prediction framework to predict the average GPU power, maximum GPU utilization, and maximum GPU memory utilization values of heterogeneous HPC system applications to enable more efficient scheduling decisions and power-aware system operation. Our prediction framework consists of two stages: 1) using only the Slurm accounting logs as training data and 2) augmenting the training data with historical GPU profiling metrics collected with DCGM. The maximum GPU utilization predictions using only the Slurm submission features achieve up to 97% accuracy. Furthermore, features engineered from GPU-compute and memory activity metrics exhibit good correlations with average power utilization, and our runtime power usage prediction experiments result in up to 92% prediction accuracy. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of DCGM metrics in capturing application characteristics and highlight their potential for developing predictive models to support dynamic power management in HPC systems.

</details>


### [12] [What can be computed in average anonymous networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02192)
*Joel Rybicki,Oleg Verbitsky,Maksim Zhukovskii*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在极弱的匿名分布式计算模型（SB和MB模型）中，如何利用随机图的结构特性突破匿名性的限制。主要发现在于：在这些弱模型下，仍能在随机图上以高概率计算唯一标识符，从而使得弱模型的计算能力几乎等同于强力的LOCAL模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索分布式计算模型的理论边界。SB和MB模型（节点匿名、仅能广播接收消息）严格弱于标准的LOCAL模型，通常认为计算能力极其有限。作者希望探究：在输入图具有随机性（如$G(n,p)$）这一自然假设下，这些弱模型是否能够突破其理论限制，完成更复杂的计算任务？这有助于理解随机性对分布式计算能力的影响。

Method: 核心方法受启发于图同构测试中的规范标号技术。作者设计了一轮确定性SB算法，使用$O(\log n)$位消息。该算法利用了随机图$G(n,p)$中节点局部邻域结构以高概率具有唯一性这一特性，通过比较节点的局部视图来生成唯一标识符，从而“模拟”出节点身份。该方法随后被用作通用转换工具，将现有的非匿名算法适配到匿名网络中。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对于$n^{\varepsilon-1} \le p \le 1/2$的$G(n,p)$随机图，提出的算法能以高概率生成唯一标识符；(2) 基于此，给出了在$O(1/\varepsilon)$轮内找到三角形的匿名算法；(3) 建立了随机图环境下“蒙特卡洛”与“拉斯维加斯”类分布式算法的计算层级关系，并证明了模型层级的坍塌：在绝大多数输入图上，SB模型的计算能力等同于LOCAL模型。

Conclusion: 结论表明，在随机输入图的假设下，分布式计算模型之间的强弱界限变得模糊。虽然SB模型在理论上是严格弱于LOCAL模型的，但在随机图上，其计算能力几乎与LOCAL模型相当。这一发现不仅展示了随机图结构在打破匿名性限制中的作用，也为在极弱模型上设计高效分布式算法提供了新的思路和理论依据。

Abstract: We study what deterministic distributed algorithms can compute on random input graphs in extremely weak models of distributed computing: all nodes are anonymous, and in each communication round, nodes broadcast a message to all their neighbors, receive a (multi)set of messages from their neighbors, and update their local state. These correspond to the SB and MB models introduced by Hella et al. [PODC 2012] and are strictly weaker than the standard port-numbering PN and LOCAL models.
  We investigate what can be computed almost surely on random input graphs. We give a one-round deterministic SB-algorithm using $O(\log n)$-bit messages that computes unique identifiers with high probability on anonymous networks sampled from $G(n,p)$, where $n^{\varepsilon-1} \le p \le 1/2$ and $\varepsilon>0$ is an arbitrarily small constant. This algorithm is inspired by canonical labeling techniques in graph isomorphism testing and can be used to "anonymize" existing distributed graph algorithms designed for the broadcast CONGEST and LOCAL models. In particular, we give a new anonymous algorithm that finds a triangle in $O(1/\varepsilon)$ rounds on the above input distribution.
  We also investigate computational power of natural analogs of "Monte Carlo" and "Las Vegas" distributed graph algorithms in the random graph setting, and establish some new collapse and hierarchy results. For example, our work shows the collapse of the weak model hierarchy of Hella et al. on $G(n,p)$, as apart from a vanishingly small fraction of input graphs, the SB model is as powerful as LOCAL.

</details>

### [13] [DySCo: Dynamic Semantic Compression for Effective Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01261)
*Xiang Ao,Yinyu Tan,Mengru Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DySCo框架，通过熵引导动态采样和层次频率增强分解解决长序列预测中的噪声与冗余问题，提升了模型性能并降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间序列预测任务中，延长回看窗口虽能提供更丰富的历史上下文，但在实际应用中往往引入无关噪声和计算冗余，导致模型难以有效捕捉复杂的长期依赖关系。

Method: 提出了动态语义压缩框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 熵引导动态采样机制，自主识别并保留高熵片段，压缩冗余趋势；2) 层次频率增强分解策略，分离高频异常与低频模式，确保稀疏采样时保留关键细节；3) 跨尺度交互混合器，动态融合全局上下文与局部表示。

Result: 实验结果表明，DySCo作为一个通用的即插即用模块，能够显著增强主流模型捕捉长期相关性的能力，同时降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: DySCo框架有效解决了长序列预测中的噪声干扰和计算冗余问题，成功实现了在降低计算成本的同时提升模型对长期依赖的捕捉能力，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) is critical across domains such as finance, meteorology, and energy. While extending the lookback window theoretically provides richer historical context, in practice, it often introduces irrelevant noise and computational redundancy, preventing models from effectively capturing complex long-term dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose a Dynamic Semantic Compression (DySCo) framework. Unlike traditional methods that rely on fixed heuristics, DySCo introduces an Entropy-Guided Dynamic Sampling (EGDS) mechanism to autonomously identify and retain high-entropy segments while compressing redundant trends. Furthermore, we incorporate a Hierarchical Frequency-Enhanced Decomposition (HFED) strategy to separate high-frequency anomalies from low-frequency patterns, ensuring that critical details are preserved during sparse sampling. Finally, a Cross-Scale Interaction Mixer(CSIM) is designed to dynamically fuse global contexts with local representations, replacing simple linear aggregation. Experimental results demonstrate that DySCo serves as a universal plug-and-play module, significantly enhancing the ability of mainstream models to capture long-term correlations with reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [14] [Sven: Singular Value Descent as a Computationally Efficient Natural Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01279)
*Samuel Bright-Thonney,Thomas R. Harvey,Andre Lukas,Jesse Thaler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sven算法，利用损失函数的逐点分解特性，通过截断奇异值分解近似计算Moore-Penrose伪逆，以最小范数参数更新同时满足所有数据点的残差条件，在回归任务上超越Adam且比LBFGS更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化方法将全部损失函数简化为单一标量后计算参数更新，忽略了损失函数天然分解为各数据点残差之和的结构；传统自然梯度方法计算复杂度随参数量平方增长，难以扩展。

Method: 将每个数据点的残差视为需同时满足的独立条件，使用损失Jacobian矩阵的Moore-Penrose伪逆寻找最小范数参数更新；通过截断奇异值分解仅保留k个最重要方向来近似伪逆，计算开销仅为SGD的k倍；可理解为过参数化条件下自然梯度方法的推广。

Result: 在回归任务上显著优于Adam等一阶方法，收敛更快且最终损失更低；在计算时间上相比LBFGS保持竞争力但成本更低；在欠参数化极限下可恢复为自然梯度下降。

Conclusion: Sven算法在保持较低计算开销的同时有效利用了损失函数结构，适用于标准机器学习基准测试及科学计算中需要满足多条件的自定义损失函数场景；主要扩展瓶颈为内存开销，并提出了相应的缓解策略。

Abstract: We introduce Sven (Singular Value dEsceNt), a new optimization algorithm for neural networks that exploits the natural decomposition of loss functions into a sum over individual data points, rather than reducing the full loss to a single scalar before computing a parameter update. Sven treats each data point's residual as a separate condition to be satisfied simultaneously, using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse of the loss Jacobian to find the minimum-norm parameter update that best satisfies all conditions at once. In practice, this pseudoinverse is approximated via a truncated singular value decomposition, retaining only the $k$ most significant directions and incurring a computational overhead of only a factor of $k$ relative to stochastic gradient descent. This is in comparison to traditional natural gradient methods, which scale as the square of the number of parameters. We show that Sven can be understood as a natural gradient method generalized to the over-parametrized regime, recovering natural gradient descent in the under-parametrized limit. On regression tasks, Sven significantly outperforms standard first-order methods including Adam, converging faster and to a lower final loss, while remaining competitive with LBFGS at a fraction of the wall-time cost. We discuss the primary challenge to scaling, namely memory overhead, and propose mitigation strategies. Beyond standard machine learning benchmarks, we anticipate that Sven will find natural application in scientific computing settings where custom loss functions decompose into several conditions.

</details>


### [15] [Forecasting Supply Chain Disruptions with Foresight Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01298)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个端到端框架，通过使用已发生的供应链中断结果作为监督信号来训练大语言模型（LLM），使其能够生成校准的概率预测，性能显著优于包括GPT-5在内的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测供应链中断是企业和政策制定者面临的核心挑战。主要难点在于如何从嘈杂、非结构化的输入中，对发生频率低但影响巨大的事件进行可靠推理，而通用模型在没有针对特定任务适配的情况下表现不佳。

Method: 引入了一个端到端框架，利用已实现的供应链中断结果作为监督，训练LLM生成校准的概率预测，而非依赖显式提示工程。

Result: 训练后的模型在准确性、校准度和精确度方面均显著优于包括GPT-5在内的强基线模型；训练过程促使模型在没有显式提示的情况下形成了更具结构化和可靠性的概率推理能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果为训练能够生成决策就绪信号的领域特定预测模型提供了一条通用路径，并开源了评估数据集以促进透明度。

Abstract: Anticipating supply chain disruptions before they materialize is a core challenge for firms and policymakers alike. A key difficulty is learning to reason reliably about infrequent, high-impact events from noisy and unstructured inputs - a setting where general-purpose models struggle without task-specific adaptation. We introduce an end-to-end framework that trains LLMs to produce calibrated probabilistic forecasts using realized disruption outcomes as supervision. The resulting model substantially outperforms strong baselines - including GPT-5 - on accuracy, calibration, and precision. We also show that training induces more structured and reliable probabilistic reasoning without explicit prompting. These results suggest a general pathway for training domain-specific forecasting models that produce decision-ready signals. To support transparency we open-source the evaluation dataset used in this study.
  Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LightningRodLabs/supply-chain-predictions

</details>


### [16] [Taming the Exponential: A Fast Softmax Surrogate for Integer-Native Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02292)
*Dimitrios Danopoulos,Enrico Lupi,Michael Kagan,Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HCCS方法，用限幅线性映射替代Transformer中的指数softmax，支持int8优化，在AMD AI Engines上显著提升吞吐量且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer多头注意力中的softmax在小型模型低精度推理时成为计算瓶颈，指数和归一化操作开销大；AMD Versal AI Engines现有实现（bfloat16或LUT）未能充分利用高吞吐整数向量处理单元。

Method: 提出Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS)：一种有界、单调的指数softmax替代方案，对最大值中心的注意力logits使用限幅线性映射；为每个注意力头引入轻量级校准参数，基于代表性数据集离线优化以保持各头统计特性；针对AMD AI Engines的int8 MAC单元设计硬件实现。

Result: HCCS生成稳定的概率分布，保持原始logits顺序且值非负；在AMD Versal AI Engines上提供到int8 MAC单元的自然映射；显著超越其他参考实现的速度性能，在小型或重度量化MHA工作负载上经量化感知重训练后保持竞争力任务精度；是首个针对AMD AI Engines的int8优化softmax替代方案。

Conclusion: HCCS为低精度推理场景提供了一种高效softmax替代方案，成功解决了传统softmax在特定硬件平台的计算瓶颈，实现了速度与精度的良好平衡，为Transformer模型在边缘AI加速器上的部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Softmax can become a computational bottleneck in the Transformer model's Multi-Head Attention (MHA) block, particularly in small models under low-precision inference, where exponentiation and normalization incur significant overhead. As such, we suggest using Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS), a bounded, monotone surrogate to the exponential softmax function, which uses a clipped linear mapping of the max centered attention logits. This approximation produces a stable probability distribution, maintains the ordering of the original logits and has non-negative values. HCCS differs from previous softmax surrogates as it includes a set of lightweight calibration parameters that are optimized offline based on a representative dataset and calibrated for each individual attention head to preserve the statistical properties of the individual heads. We describe a hardware-motivated implementation of HCCS for high-throughput scenarios targeting the AMD Versal AI Engines. The current reference implementations from AMD for this platform rely upon either bfloat16 arithmetic or LUTs to perform the exponential operation, which might limit the throughput of the platform and fail to utilize the high-throughput integer vector processing units of the AI Engine. In contrast, HCCS provides a natural mapping to the AI Engines' int8 multiply accumulate (MAC) units. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first int8 optimized softmax surrogate for AMD AI engines that significantly exceeds the speed performance of other reference implementations while maintaining competitive task accuracy on small or heavily quantized MHA workloads after quantization-aware retraining.

</details>


### [17] [UQ-SHRED: uncertainty quantification of shallow recurrent decoder networks for sparse sensing via engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01305)
*Mars Liyao Gao,Yuxuan Bao,Amy S. Rude,Xinwei Shen,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UQ-SHRED框架，通过引入engression分布回归方法，为稀疏传感重建提供了不确定性量化能力，在保持低计算开销的同时实现了校准良好的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SHRED架构在从稀疏传感器测量重建高维时空场方面表现出色，但在复杂、数据稀缺、高频或随机系统中，无法对重建结果的不确定性进行有效估计，限制了其在关键科学应用中的可靠性。

Method: 提出UQ-SHRED框架，采用基于神经网络的分布回归方法engression，通过学习以传感器历史为条件的空间状态预测分布来建模不确定性。具体实现包括：在传感器输入中注入随机噪声、使用能量分数损失函数训练，仅需单次架构通过重采样即可生成预测分布，无需重新训练或额外网络结构。

Result: 在湍流、大气动力学、神经科学和天体物理学等复杂合成及真实数据集上，UQ-SHRED能够提供分布近似并生成校准良好的置信区间。消融研究进一步验证了各模型设置对性能的影响及其在不同实验设置下的有效性。

Conclusion: UQ-SHRED成功地将不确定性量化集成到稀疏传感重建框架中，以最小的计算开销解决了SHRED的关键局限性，为科学应用中的高维时空场重建提供了可靠的不确定性估计能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-dimensional spatiotemporal fields from sparse sensor measurements is critical in a wide range of scientific applications. The SHallow REcurrent Decoder (SHRED) architecture is a recent state-of-the-art architecture that reconstructs high-quality spatial domain from hyper-sparse sensor measurement streams. An important limitation of SHRED is that in complex, data-scarce, high-frequency, or stochastic systems, portions of the spatiotemporal field must be modeled with valid uncertainty estimation. We introduce UQ-SHRED, a distributional learning framework for sparse sensing problems that provides uncertainty quantification through a neural network-based distributional regression called engression. UQ-SHRED models the uncertainty by learning the predictive distribution of the spatial state conditioned on the sensor history. By injecting stochastic noise into sensor inputs and training with an energy score loss, UQ-SHRED produces predictive distributions with minimal computational overhead, requiring only noise injection at the input and resampling through a single architecture without retraining or additional network structures. On complicated synthetic and real-life datasets including turbulent flow, atmospheric dynamics, neuroscience and astrophysics, UQ-SHRED provides a distributional approximation with well-calibrated confidence intervals. We further conduct ablation studies to understand how each model setting affects the quality of the UQ-SHRED performance, and its validity on uncertainty quantification over a set of different experimental setups.

</details>

Method: CuTeGen是一个基于代理的自动化框架，将内核开发视为结构化的“生成-测试-精炼”工作流。其核心设计包括：1) 使用CuTe抽象层生成内核，该层暴露了平铺和数据移动等性能关键结构，同时为迭代修改提供稳定表示；2) 采用执行验证、结构化调试和分阶段优化来渐进式精炼单个内核；3) 引入负载感知的优化提示和延迟整合性能分析反馈来指导性能提升。

Result: 在矩阵乘法和激活函数工作负载上的实验结果表明，CuTeGen能够生成功能正确的GPU内核，并且其性能与优化后的库实现相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CuTeGen通过渐进式精炼单个内核的策略，证明了自动化GPU内核生成可以在保证正确性的前提下达到与手工优化库相当的性能水平，为解决GPU内核开发的高门槛问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are critical to modern machine learning systems, yet developing efficient implementations remains a challenging, expert-driven process due to the tight coupling between algorithmic structure, memory hierarchy usage, and hardware-specific optimizations. Recent work has explored using large language models (LLMs) to generate GPU kernels automatically, but generated implementations often struggle to maintain correctness and achieve competitive performance across iterative refinements. We present CuTeGen, an agentic framework for automated generation and optimization of GPU kernels that treats kernel development as a structured generate--test--refine workflow. Unlike approaches that rely on one-shot generation or large-scale search over candidate implementations, CuTeGen focuses on progressive refinement of a single evolving kernel through execution-based validation, structured debugging, and staged optimization. A key design choice is to generate kernels using the CuTe abstraction layer, which exposes performance-critical structures such as tiling and data movement while providing a more stable representation for iterative modification. To guide performance improvement, CuTeGen incorporates workload-aware optimization prompts and delayed integration of profiling feedback. Experimental results on matrix multiplication and activation workloads demonstrate that the framework produces functionally correct kernels and achieves competitive performance relative to optimized library implementations.

</details>


### [19] [An Online Machine Learning Multi-resolution Optimization Framework for Energy System Design Limit of Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01308)
*Oluwamayowa O. Amusat,Luka Grbcic,Remi Patureau,M. Jibran S. Zuberi,Dan Gunter,Michael Wetter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器学习加速的多分辨率优化框架，用于工业综合能源系统的设计与验证，在减少42%的架构-运行性能差距的同时，降低了34%的高保真模型评估需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程综合能源系统的可靠设计需要跨多个保真度（从架构级定尺寸到高保真动态运行）的优化与验证。然而，不同保真度模型间的失配模糊了性能损失的来源，使得架构到运行的性能差距难以量化，且高保真模型评估成本昂贵。

Method: 提出在线机器学习加速的多分辨率优化框架。首先进行多目标架构优化确定系统配置与组件容量；然后开发ML加速的多分辨率滚动时域最优控制策略，基于预测不确定性自适应调度优化分辨率，并利用精英低保真解为高保真求解提供热启动。

Result: 在1 MW工业热负荷中试能源系统上验证表明：相比基于规则的控制器，多分辨率策略将架构-运行性能差距减少达42%；相比无ML引导的多保真方法，高保真模型评估需求降低34%，实现了更快更可靠的设计验证。

Conclusion: 该框架使高保真验证变得可处理，为可实现的运行性能提供了实际上界，成功解决了跨保真度模型失配与评估成本高昂的关键挑战，为工业能源系统的可靠设计提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Designing reliable integrated energy systems for industrial processes requires optimization and verification models across multiple fidelities, from architecture-level sizing to high-fidelity dynamic operation. However, model mismatch across fidelities obscures the sources of performance loss and complicates the quantification of architecture-to-operation performance gaps. We propose an online, machine-learning-accelerated multi-resolution optimization framework that estimates an architecture-specific upper bound on achievable performance while minimizing expensive high-fidelity model evaluations. We demonstrate the approach on a pilot energy system supplying a 1 MW industrial heat load. First, we solve a multi-objective architecture optimization to select the system configuration and component capacities. We then develop an machine learning (ML)-accelerated multi-resolution, receding-horizon optimal control strategy that approaches the achievable-performance bound for the specified architecture, given the additional controls and dynamics not captured by the architectural optimization model. The ML-guided controller adaptively schedules the optimization resolution based on predictive uncertainty and warm-starts high-fidelity solves using elite low-fidelity solutions. Our results on the pilot case study show that the proposed multi-resolution strategy reduces the architecture-to-operation performance gap by up to 42% relative to a rule-based controller, while reducing required high-fidelity model evaluations by 34% relative to the same multi-fidelity approach without ML guidance, enabling faster and more reliable design verification. Together, these gains make high-fidelity verification tractable, providing a practical upper bound on achievable operational performance.

</details>


### [20] [FourierMoE: Fourier Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01762)
*Juyong Jiang,Fan Wang,Hong Qi,Sunghun Kim,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FourierMoE，一种将混合专家架构与逆离散傅里叶变换（IDFT）相结合的频域参数高效微调方法。该方法利用频率自适应路由器将词元分配至不同频段的专家，在保证无损重建的同时，以更少的可训练参数在多任务和单任务设置中均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在多任务场景下面临任务干扰和表征能力不足的问题；近期引入混合专家的方法主要在空间域操作，存在结构冗余和参数开销。作者旨在通过将适配过程重构至频域来克服这些限制。

Method: 首先进行频谱分析，发现不同任务呈现独特的频率能量分布，且LLM层具有异构的频率敏感性。基于此，提出FourierMoE：将MoE架构与逆离散傅里叶变换（IDFT）结合，采用频率自适应路由器将词元分发至专精于不同频段的专家。每个专家学习一组共轭对称复系数，保留完整的相位和幅度信息，并在理论上保证无损IDFT重建为实数值空间权重。

Result: 在28个基准测试、多种模型架构和规模上进行了广泛评估。实验结果表明，FourierMoE在单任务和多任务设置中均持续优于竞争基线，同时使用了显著更少的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了频域专家适配作为大语言模型微调的一种有效且参数高效范式的潜力，为解决多任务场景下的任务干扰和参数效率问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for adapting large language models (LLMs) under constrained computational budgets. However, standard PEFT methods often struggle in multi-task fine-tuning settings, where diverse optimization objectives induce task interference and limited parameter budgets lead to representational deficiency. While recent approaches incorporate mixture-of-experts (MoE) to alleviate these issues, they predominantly operate in the spatial domain, which may introduce structural redundancy and parameter overhead. To overcome these limitations, we reformulate adaptation in the spectral domain. Our spectral analysis reveals that different tasks exhibit distinct frequency energy distributions, and that LLM layers display heterogeneous frequency sensitivities. Motivated by these insights, we propose FourierMoE, which integrates the MoE architecture with the inverse discrete Fourier transform (IDFT) for frequency-aware adaptation. Specifically, FourierMoE employs a frequency-adaptive router to dispatch tokens to experts specialized in distinct frequency bands. Each expert learns a set of conjugate-symmetric complex coefficients, preserving complete phase and amplitude information while theoretically guaranteeing lossless IDFT reconstruction into real-valued spatial weights. Extensive evaluations across 28 benchmarks, multiple model architectures, and scales demonstrate that FourierMoE consistently outperforms competitive baselines in both single-task and multi-task settings while using significantly fewer trainable parameters. These results highlight the promise of spectral-domain expert adaptation as an effective and parameter-efficient paradigm for LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [21] [JetPrism: diagnosing convergence for generative simulation and inverse problems in nuclear physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01313)
*Zeyu Xia,Tyler Kim,Trevor Reed,Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox,Adam Szczepaniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了条件流匹配(CFM)的标准损失函数在严谨物理应用中存在误导性，通过JetPrism框架验证了物理信息指标在损失收敛后仍持续改进，提出了多指标评估协议以确保生成模型的物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真蒙特卡洛模拟和复杂逆问题（如探测器去弥散）计算成本高昂，CFM虽能加速这些任务，但其标准训练损失在物理应用中会过早收敛且无法真实反映物理保真度，需要建立更可靠的评估体系。

Method: 开发了JetPrism可配置CFM框架作为生成代理，使用合成压力测试和Jefferson Lab运动学数据集($γp \to ρ^0 p \to π^+π^- p$)，设计了包含边缘和成对$χ^2$统计、$W_1$距离、相关矩阵距离($D_{\mathrm{corr}}$)和最近邻距离比($R_{\mathrm{NN}}$)的多指标评估协议。

Result: 实验证明标准CFM损失收敛后，物理信息指标仍持续显著改进，验证了传统损失作为收敛指示器的不可靠性；JetPrism在确保与真值数据精确统计一致性的同时避免了训练集记忆问题。

Conclusion: 领域特定评估必须优先于通用损失指标，JetPrism作为可靠的生成代理确保了高保真模拟，该诊断框架可扩展至医学成像、天体物理、半导体发现和量化金融等领域的参数生成和复杂逆问题。

Abstract: High-fidelity Monte Carlo simulations and complex inverse problems, such as mapping smeared experimental observations to ground-truth states, are computationally intensive yet essential for robust data analysis. Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) offers a mathematically robust approach to accelerating these tasks, but we demonstrate its standard training loss is fundamentally misleading. In rigorous physics applications, CFM loss plateaus prematurely, serving as an unreliable indicator of true convergence and physical fidelity. To investigate this disconnect, we designed JetPrism, a configurable CFM framework acting as an efficient generative surrogate for evaluating unconditional generation and conditional detector unfolding. Using synthetic stress tests and a Jefferson Lab kinematic dataset ($γp \to ρ^0 p \to π^+π^- p$) relevant to the forthcoming Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), we establish that physics-informed metrics continue to improve significantly long after the standard loss converges. Consequently, we propose a multi-metric evaluation protocol incorporating marginal and pairwise $χ^2$ statistics, $W_1$ distances, correlation matrix distances ($D_{\mathrm{corr}}$), and nearest-neighbor distance ratios ($R_{\mathrm{NN}}$). By demonstrating that domain-specific evaluations must supersede generic loss metrics, this work establishes JetPrism as a dependable generative surrogate that ensures precise statistical agreement with ground-truth data without memorizing the training set. While demonstrated in nuclear physics, this diagnostic framework is readily extensible to parameter generation and complex inverse problems across broad domains. Potential applications span medical imaging, astrophysics, semiconductor discovery, and quantitative finance, where high-fidelity simulation, rigorous inversion, and generative reliability are critical.

</details>


### [22] [Detecting Complex Money Laundering Patterns with Incremental and Distributed Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01315)
*Haseeb Tariq,Alen Kaja,Marwan Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDiRect框架，通过模糊划分大规模交易图并分布式处理，在无监督设置下提升反洗钱检测的效率与准确性，有效降低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反洗钱检测算法在处理规模和复杂性方面存在困难，且基于规则的系统会产生大量误报。洗钱者通过模拟正常交易模式规避监控，使得犯罪资金难以被发现。

Method: 提出ReDiRect框架，核心方法是在无监督设置下，将大规模交易图模糊划分为较小且可管理的组件，从而实现快速的分布式处理，并定义了更精确的评估指标以衡量检测效果。

Result: 在开源Libra数据集和IBM Watson发布的合成数据集上进行全面实验，结果显示该框架在效率和实际应用性方面优于现有及最先进技术。

Conclusion: ReDiRect框架能有效克服现有反洗钱检测方法的局限性，提高检测效率并降低误报，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Money launderers take advantage of limitations in existing detection approaches by hiding their financial footprints in a deceitful manner. They manage this by replicating transaction patterns that the monitoring systems cannot easily distinguish. As a result, criminally gained assets are pushed into legitimate financial channels without drawing attention. Algorithms developed to monitor money flows often struggle with scale and complexity. The difficulty of identifying such activities is further intensified by the (persistent) inability of current solutions to control the excessive number of false positive signals produced by rigid, risk-based rules systems. We propose a framework called ReDiRect (REduce, DIstribute, and RECTify), specifically designed to overcome these challenges. The primary contribution of our work is a novel framing of this problem in an unsupervised setting; where a large transaction graph is fuzzily partitioned into smaller, manageable components to enable fast processing in a distributed manner. In addition, we define a refined evaluation metric that better captures the effectiveness of exposed money laundering patterns. Through comprehensive experimentation, we demonstrate that our framework achieves superior performance compared to existing and state-of-the-art techniques, particularly in terms of efficiency and real-world applicability. For validation, we used the real (open source) Libra dataset and the recently released synthetic datasets by IBM Watson. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/mhaseebtariq/redirect.

</details>


### [23] [Efficient and Principled Scientific Discovery through Bayesian Optimization: A Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01328)
*Zhongwei Yu,Rasul Tutunov,Alexandre Max Maraval,Zikai Xie,Zhenzhi Tan,Jiankang Wang,Zijing Li,Liangliang Xu,Qi Yang,Jun Jiang,Sanzhong Luo,Zhenxiao Guo,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程介绍了贝叶斯优化（BO）如何通过形式化和自动化传统的“假设-实验-修正”科学循环，替代直觉式的试错过程，从而在催化、材料科学、有机合成和分子发现等领域实现更高效、更严谨的科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的科学发现依赖于迭代式的“假设-实验-修正”循环，虽然推动了数百年的进步，但其依赖直觉和临时的实施方式往往导致资源浪费、设计效率低下，并可能遗漏关键见解。为了解决这些局限性，需要一个更加原理化、自动化的框架来指导实验设计。

Method: 将科学发现构建为一个优化问题，引入贝叶斯优化（BO）框架。该方法利用代理模型（如高斯过程）将经验观察建模为演化的假设，并利用采集函数指导实验选择，在利用已知知识和探索未知领域之间取得平衡。此外，还涵盖了面向科学应用的关键技术扩展，包括批量实验、异方差性、上下文优化和人机协同集成。

Result: 通过在催化、材料科学、有机合成和分子发现等领域的真实案例研究，展示了贝叶斯优化工作流程在端到端科学实验中的实际有效性。该教程为跨学科研究人员提供了分层内容，成功连接了人工智能领域的BO进展与自然科学实践应用。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯优化能够消除科学实验中的猜测和人工试错，赋予跨学科研究人员设计更高效实验的能力，从而加速有理有据的科学发现进程。

Abstract: Traditional scientific discovery relies on an iterative hypothesise-experiment-refine cycle that has driven progress for centuries, but its intuitive, ad-hoc implementation often wastes resources, yields inefficient designs, and misses critical insights. This tutorial presents Bayesian Optimisation (BO), a principled probability-driven framework that formalises and automates this core scientific cycle. BO uses surrogate models (e.g., Gaussian processes) to model empirical observations as evolving hypotheses, and acquisition functions to guide experiment selection, balancing exploitation of known knowledge and exploration of uncharted domains to eliminate guesswork and manual trial-and-error. We first frame scientific discovery as an optimisation problem, then unpack BO's core components, end-to-end workflows, and real-world efficacy via case studies in catalysis, materials science, organic synthesis, and molecule discovery. We also cover critical technical extensions for scientific applications, including batched experimentation, heteroscedasticity, contextual optimisation, and human-in-the-loop integration. Tailored for a broad audience, this tutorial bridges AI advances in BO with practical natural science applications, offering tiered content to empower cross-disciplinary researchers to design more efficient experiments and accelerate principled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [24] [Model Merging via Data-Free Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01329)
*Marawan Gamal Abdel Hameed,Derek Tam,Pascal Jr Tikeng Notsawo,Colin Raffel,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需数据且低计算成本的方法来估计协方差矩阵，从而有效地将模型合并问题转化为层间优化问题，在多个任务中实现了优于现有无数据合并方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并方法分为两类：一类是启发式方法，虽无需数据但缺乏理论支撑；另一类是基于干扰最小化的优化框架，虽有理论依据但需要数据来估计协方差矩阵，限制了其实用性。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，使优化框架也能摆脱对数据的依赖。

Method: 本文重新审视了干扰最小化框架，发现在特定条件下，可以直接通过差分矩阵来估计逐层协方差矩阵，从而无需使用辅助数据，同时降低了计算成本。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务的多个基准测试上进行了验证，模型规模涵盖8600万到70亿参数。实验结果表明，该方法优于此前最先进的无数据模型合并方法。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将理论扎实的优化框架与无需数据的实用性相结合，为模型合并领域提供了一个高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Model merging provides a way of cheaply combining individual models to produce a model that inherits each individual's capabilities. While some merging methods can approach the performance of multitask training, they are often heuristically motivated and lack theoretical justification. A principled alternative is to pose model merging as a layer-wise optimization problem that directly minimizes interference between tasks. However, this formulation requires estimating per-layer covariance matrices from data, which may not be available when performing merging. In contrast, many of the heuristically-motivated methods do not require auxiliary data, making them practically advantageous. In this work, we revisit the interference minimization framework and show that, under certain conditions, covariance matrices can be estimated directly from difference matrices, eliminating the need for data while also reducing computational costs. We validate our approach across vision and language benchmarks on models ranging from 86M parameters to 7B parameters, outperforming previous data-free state-of-the-art merging methods

</details>


### [25] [SECURE: Stable Early Collision Understanding via Robust Embeddings in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01337)

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 揭示了现有事故预测模型面对微小扰动时的脆弱性，提出了SECURE框架，通过多目标损失函数增强模型鲁棒性，在提升抗干扰能力的同时实现了新的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习显著推进了事故预测的发展，但现有最先进模型（如CRASH）在面对现实世界中的微小输入扰动时表现出显著的预测不稳定性和潜在表示不稳定性，这对安全关键系统的可靠性构成了严重风险。

Method: 提出SECURE框架，该框架基于预测空间和潜在特征空间的一致性与稳定性四个关键属性。采用原则性训练方法，通过多目标损失函数对基线模型进行微调：最小化与参考模型的散度，并惩罚对对抗性扰动的敏感性。

Result: 在DAD和CCD数据集上的实验表明，该方法不仅显著增强了针对各种扰动的鲁棒性，还提升了在干净数据上的性能，达到了新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: 通过形式化定义并强制执行模型鲁棒性约束，SECURE框架有效解决了安全关键系统中深度学习模型对输入扰动敏感的问题，证明了增强鲁棒性与提升性能可以同时实现。

Abstract: While deep learning has significantly advanced accident anticipation, the robustness of these safety-critical systems against real-world perturbations remains a major challenge. We reveal that state-of-the-art models like CRASH, despite their high performance, exhibit significant instability in predictions and latent representations when faced with minor input perturbations, posing serious reliability risks. To address this, we introduce SECURE - Stable Early Collision Understanding Robust Embeddings, a framework that formally defines and enforces model robustness. SECURE is founded on four key attributes: consistency and stability in both prediction space and latent feature space. We propose a principled training methodology that fine-tunes a baseline model using a multi-objective loss, which minimizes divergence from a reference model and penalizes sensitivity to adversarial perturbations. Experiments on DAD and CCD datasets demonstrate that our approach not only significantly enhances robustness against various perturbations but also improves performance on clean data, achieving new state-of-the-art results.

</details>

*Ahmer Raza,Hudson Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于并行前缀扫描的算法，将线性指数Hawkes过程的极大似然估计从$O(N^2)$降至近似$O(N/P)$，在GPU上实现了数量级的加速，且无需任何近似假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元Hawkes过程的极大似然估计计算复杂度高达$O(N^2)$，虽然现有研究将其降至$O(N)$，但采用串行递归计算，无法利用现代GPU进行并行加速。

Method: 将Hawkes过程的强度表示为稀疏转移矩阵的乘积，利用线性时间结合律乘法，通过并行前缀扫描计算；引入批处理方案以保持恒定内存使用，避免GPU内存限制。

Result: 算法在模拟和真实数据集上实现了数量级的加速，可扩展至数千节点和数千万事件，且计算精确似然函数，无需额外假设或近似。

Conclusion: 该方法简单且高度可并行化，显著提升了计算效率，保持了模型的简单性和可解释性，并提供了开源PyTorch库实现相关优化。

Abstract: Multivariate Hawkes processes are a widely used class of self-exciting point processes, but maximum likelihood estimation naively scales as $O(N^2)$ in the number of events. The canonical linear exponential Hawkes process admits a faster $O(N)$ recurrence, but prior work evaluates this recurrence sequentially, without exploiting parallelization on modern GPUs. We show that the Hawkes process intensity can be expressed as a product of sparse transition matrices admitting a linear-time associative multiply, enabling computation via a parallel prefix scan. This yields a simple yet massively parallelizable algorithm for maximum likelihood estimation of linear exponential Hawkes processes. Our method reduces the computational complexity to approximately $O(N/P)$ with $P$ parallel processors, and naturally yields a batching scheme to maintain constant memory usage, avoiding GPU memory constraints. Importantly, it computes the exact likelihood without any additional assumptions or approximations, preserving the simplicity and interpretability of the model. We demonstrate orders-of-magnitude speedups on simulated and real datasets, scaling to thousands of nodes and tens of millions of events, substantially beyond scales reported in prior work. We provide an open-source PyTorch library implementing our optimizations.

</details>

*Vikram Krishnamurthy,Luke Snow*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应逆向强化学习（IRL）旨在通过被动观测前向学习者在强化学习过程中的梯度来重构其损失函数。然而，核心难点在于所需的梯度是反事实的，即它们以前向学习者轨迹中概率为零的事件为条件，导致朴素蒙特卡洛估计器效率极低，而常用的核平滑方法收敛缓慢。

Method: 作者提出了一种被动朗之万算法，通过引入Malliavin微积分技术来解决反事实梯度估计问题。具体而言，将反事实条件重新表述为涉及Malliavin量的无条件期望之比，从而恢复标准的估计速率。研究推导了一般朗之万结构所需的Malliavin导数及其伴随Skorohod积分形式，并提出了具体的算法方案来利用这些工具进行反事实梯度估计。

Result: 该方法成功实现了自适应IRL中的反事实梯度高效估计，恢复了标准估计速率，克服了朴素蒙特卡洛方法低效和核平滑方法收敛慢的缺陷，并通过Malliavin导数和Skorohod积分提供了具体的算法实现。

Conclusion: 通过将Malliavin微积分应用于自适应IRL中的反事实梯度估计，本文提出的方法有效解决了传统估计器效率低下的问题，为被动观测场景下的损失函数重构提供了新的理论工具和算法框架。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) recovers the loss function of a forward learner from its observed responses adaptive IRL aims to reconstruct the loss function of a forward learner by passively observing its gradients as it performs reinforcement learning (RL). This paper proposes a novel passive Langevin-based algorithm that achieves adaptive IRL. The key difficulty in adaptive IRL is that the required gradients in the passive algorithm are counterfactual, that is, they are conditioned on events of probability zero under the forward learner's trajectory. Therefore, naive Monte Carlo estimators are prohibitively inefficient, and kernel smoothing, though common, suffers from slow convergence. We overcome this by employing Malliavin calculus to efficiently estimate the required counterfactual gradients. We reformulate the counterfactual conditioning as a ratio of unconditioned expectations involving Malliavin quantities, thus recovering standard estimation rates. We derive the necessary Malliavin derivatives and their adjoint Skorohod integral formulations for a general Langevin structure, and provide a concrete algorithmic approach which exploits these for counterfactual gradient estimation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了PI-JEPA框架，一种无需完整PDE求解的代理模型预训练方法，通过在未标记参数场上进行掩码潜在预测和PDE残差正则化，在仅需100个标记样本的情况下显著降低了多物理场代理模型的仿真预算需求。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油藏仿真工作流面临根本性的数据不对称问题：输入参数场（地质统计渗透率实现、孔隙度分布）可以任意生成，但现有的神经算子代理模型需要大量昂贵的标记仿真轨迹，且无法利用这些未标记数据的结构信息。

Method: 提出PI-JEPA（物理信息联合嵌入预测架构）代理预训练框架：1）无需完整PDE求解，在未标记参数场上进行掩码潜在预测；2）采用子算子级PDE残差正则化；3）预测器组与控制方程的Lie-Trotter算子分裂分解结构对齐，为每个子过程（压力、饱和度输运、反应）分配独立的物理约束潜在模块。

Result: 在单相达西流实验中，PI-JEPA在N_ℓ=100时比FNO误差降低1.9倍，比DeepONet降低2.4倍；在N_ℓ=500时比纯监督训练提升24%。仅需100个标记仿真运行即可实现有效微调。

Conclusion: 无标记代理预训练大幅降低了多物理场代理模型部署所需的仿真预算，证明了利用未标记参数场进行物理信息预训练的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Reservoir simulation workflows face a fundamental data asymmetry: input parameter fields (geostatistical permeability realizations, porosity distributions) are free to generate in arbitrary quantities, yet existing neural operator surrogates require large corpora of expensive labeled simulation trajectories and cannot exploit this unlabeled structure. We introduce \textbf{PI-JEPA} (Physics-Informed Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture), a surrogate pretraining framework that trains \emph{without any completed PDE solves}, using masked latent prediction on unlabeled parameter fields under per-sub-operator PDE residual regularization. The predictor bank is structurally aligned with the Lie--Trotter operator-splitting decomposition of the governing equations, dedicating a separate physics-constrained latent module to each sub-process (pressure, saturation transport, reaction), enabling fine-tuning with as few as 100 labeled simulation runs. On single-phase Darcy flow, PI-JEPA achieves $1.9\times$ lower error than FNO and $2.4\times$ lower error than DeepONet at $N_\ell{=}100$, with 24\% improvement over supervised-only training at $N_\ell{=}500$, demonstrating that label-free surrogate pretraining substantially reduces the simulation budget required for multiphysics surrogate deployment.

</details>


### [29] [Residuals-based Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01378)
*Qing Zhu,Xian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于残差的离线强化学习框架，通过在贝尔曼最优算子中显式引入转移动力学估计误差，有效解决了数据覆盖受限和分布偏移问题，并提供了理论保证和有效的深度Q学习算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习方法通常依赖于对数据覆盖的严格假设，且容易受到分布偏移的影响，这在很大程度上限制了其在高风险实际应用中的性能和适用性。因此，作者希望开发一种能够放宽数据覆盖假设并更好地处理估计误差的通用框架。

Method: 作者提出了一个基于残差的离线强化学习框架，适用于一般的状体和动作空间。核心方法是定义了一个基于残差的贝尔曼最优算子，该算子利用经验残差显式地将转移动力学学习中的估计误差纳入策略优化过程中。作者证明了该贝尔曼算子是收缩映射，并推导了其不动点的渐近最优性和有限样本保证。在此基础上，进一步开发了基于残差的离线深度Q学习（DQN）算法。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的基于残差的贝尔曼算子具有收缩性质，其不动点在特定条件下具有渐近最优性以及有限样本保证。在随机CartPole环境中的实验结果验证了所提出的基于残差的离线DQN算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了一个基于残差的离线强化学习理论框架，不仅放宽了对数据覆盖的限制性假设，还为处理估计误差提供了理论依据，实验证明了该方法的有效性，为离线强化学习在复杂实际问题中的应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) has received increasing attention for learning policies from previously collected data without interaction with the real environment, which is particularly important in high-stakes applications. While a growing body of work has developed offline RL algorithms, these methods often rely on restrictive assumptions about data coverage and suffer from distribution shift. In this paper, we propose a residuals-based offline RL framework for general state and action spaces. Specifically, we define a residuals-based Bellman optimality operator that explicitly incorporates estimation error in learning transition dynamics into policy optimization by leveraging empirical residuals. We show that this Bellman operator is a contraction mapping and identify conditions under which its fixed point is asymptotically optimal and possesses finite-sample guarantees. We further develop a residuals-based offline deep Q-learning (DQN) algorithm. Using a stochastic CartPole environment, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our residuals-based offline DQN algorithm.

</details>

Result: 形成了一份包含六个PPG相关医学基准问题的清单，并提供了相应的基准数据集及其使用说明，为机器学习算法在PPG医学应用中的不确定性量化评估提供了标准化的基础。

Abstract: This report is part of the Qumphy project (22HLT01 Qumphy) that is funded by the European Union and is dedicated to the development of measures to quantify the uncertainties associated with Machine Learning algorithms applied to medical problems, in particular the analysis and processing of Photoplethysmography (PPG) signals. In this report, a list of six medical problems that are related to PPG signals and serve as Benchmark Problems is given. Suitable Benchmark datasets and their usage are described also.

</details>


### [31] [Test-Time Scaling Makes Overtraining Compute-Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01411)
*Nicholas Roberts,Sungjun Cho,Zhiqi Gao,Tzu-Heng Huang,Albert Wu,Gabriel Orlanski,Avi Trost,Kelly Buchanan,Aws Albarghouthi,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Train-to-Test (T²)扩展定律，联合优化模型大小、训练token数和推理样本数，发现在考虑推理成本后，最优预训练决策显著转向过度训练（overtraining）区域，验证了T²扩展定律的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型在测试时通过重复采样等方式扩展，推理成本随模型大小和样本数量增长，导致传统的预训练扩展定律（如Chinchilla）无法解决预训练成本与推理成本之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出Train-to-Test (T²)扩展定律，将用于测试时扩展的pass@k建模融入预训练扩展定律，在固定端到端预算约束下联合优化模型大小、训练token数和推理样本数，并考虑两种建模方式：任务损失上的联合扩展效应和任务精度上的建模影响。

Result: 在八个下游任务上的实验表明，当考虑推理成本时，最优预训练决策显著转向过度训练区域，远超标准预训练扩展套件的范围。通过在T²扩展预测的最优区域预训练过度训练模型，验证了其相比单独预训练扩展具有显著更强的性能。此外，研究发现这些结论在后训练阶段仍然有效。

Conclusion: T²扩展定律成功现代化了预训练扩展定律，使其能够处理测试时扩展场景下的联合优化问题，证明在考虑推理成本时需要转向过度训练策略，且该发现在现代部署场景（包括后训练阶段）中具有实际意义。

Abstract: Modern LLMs scale at test-time, e.g. via repeated sampling, where inference cost grows with model size and the number of samples. This creates a trade-off that pretraining scaling laws, such as Chinchilla, do not address. We present Train-to-Test ($T^2$) scaling laws that jointly optimize model size, training tokens, and number of inference samples under fixed end-to-end budgets. $T^2$ modernizes pretraining scaling laws with pass@$k$ modeling used for test-time scaling, then jointly optimizes pretraining and test-time decisions. Forecasts from $T^2$ are robust over distinct modeling approaches: measuring joint scaling effect on the task loss and modeling impact on task accuracy. Across eight downstream tasks, we find that when accounting for inference cost, optimal pretraining decisions shift radically into the overtraining regime, well-outside of the range of standard pretraining scaling suites. We validate our results by pretraining heavily overtrained models in the optimal region that $T^2$ scaling forecasts, confirming their substantially stronger performance compared to pretraining scaling alone. Finally, as frontier LLMs are post-trained, we show that our findings survive the post-training stage, making $T^2$ scaling meaningful in modern deployments.

</details>


### [32] [Improving Latent Generalization Using Test-time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01430)
*Arslan Chaudhry,Sridhar Thiagarajan,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用测试时计算（“思考”）机制，通过强化学习训练模型生成长思维链，有效提升语言模型权重知识的潜在泛化能力，且具备优于数据增强的分布外泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型存在权重内学习与上下文学习两种知识获取机制。权重内学习常面临潜在泛化能力不足的问题（如逆转诅咒），且现有训练时数据增强方案存在任务特定、扩展性差、无法泛化至分布外知识等缺陷。受上下文学习具备强大潜在泛化能力的启发，研究如何利用测试时计算来弥补权重内学习的这一根本缺陷。

Method: 采用基于正确性反馈的强化学习（RL）方法，训练模型在测试时生成长思维链。该方法使模型学会运用额外的测试时计算资源进行推理，而非依赖传统的训练时数据增强。模型通过生成与验证的迭代过程来处理权重内编码的知识。

Result: 该方法不仅有效解决了分布内知识的多种潜在泛化失败案例，且不同于数据增强基线，能泛化至未进行强化学习的分布外新知识。在纯逆转任务上，虽然思考机制无法直接解锁知识反转，但其生成-验证能力使模型表现显著优于随机猜测；然而，由于事实自验证的脆弱性，其性能仍远低于上下文学习。

Conclusion: 测试时“思考”被证实为提升语言模型权重知识潜在泛化能力的一条灵活且极具前景的技术路径，能有效弥补权重内学习在潜在泛化方面的固有缺陷。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) exhibit two distinct mechanisms for knowledge acquisition: in-weights learning (i.e., encoding information within the model weights) and in-context learning (ICL). Although these two modes offer complementary strengths, in-weights learning frequently struggles to facilitate deductive reasoning over the internalized knowledge. We characterize this limitation as a deficit in latent generalization, of which the reversal curse is one example. Conversely, in-context learning demonstrates highly robust latent generalization capabilities. To improve latent generalization from in-weights knowledge, prior approaches rely on train-time data augmentation, yet these techniques are task-specific, scale poorly, and fail to generalize to out-of-distribution knowledge. To overcome these shortcomings, this work studies how models can be taught to use test-time compute, or 'thinking', specifically to improve latent generalization. We use Reinforcement Learning (RL) from correctness feedback to train models to produce long chains-of-thought (CoTs) to improve latent generalization. Our experiments show that this thinking approach not only resolves many instances of latent generalization failures on in-distribution knowledge but also, unlike augmentation baselines, generalizes to new knowledge for which no RL was performed. Nevertheless, on pure reversal tasks, we find that thinking does not unlock direct knowledge inversion, but the generate-and-verify ability of thinking models enables them to get well above chance performance. The brittleness of factual self-verification means thinking models still remain well below the performance of in-context learning for this task. Overall, our results establish test-time thinking as a flexible and promising direction for improving the latent generalization of LMs.

</details>


### [33] [When Reward Hacking Rebounds: Understanding and Mitigating It with Representation-Level Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01476)
*Rui Wu,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM强化学习中的奖励破解现象，发现了三阶段反弹模式，并提出Advantage Modification方法，通过将捷径概念分数整合到GRPO优势计算中，有效抑制奖励破解行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的强化学习容易受到奖励破解的影响，模型通过捷径最大化奖励而不解决实际任务。需要系统研究这一现象并开发有效的检测和抑制方法。

Method: 使用环境操控设置作为受控试验台，通过表示工程从领域通用对比对中提取捷径、欺骗和评估感知的概念方向。提出Advantage Modification方法，将捷径概念分数整合到GRPO优势计算中，在策略更新前惩罚破解行为。

Result: 在两个模型上发现了可重现的三阶段反弹模式：模型首先尝试重写评估器但失败，然后暂时回归合法求解，最后采用不同策略成功破解。捷径方向最紧密地跟踪破解行为，可作为检测的有效表示代理。Advantage Modification比生成时激活转向提供更稳健的抑制效果。

Conclusion: Advantage Modification通过将惩罚内化到训练信号中而非仅在推理时应用，提供了比生成时激活转向更稳健的破解抑制效果，为LLM强化学习的安全性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for LLMs is vulnerable to reward hacking, where models exploit shortcuts to maximize reward without solving the intended task. We systematically study this phenomenon in coding tasks using an environment-manipulation setting, where models can rewrite evaluator code to trivially pass tests without solving the task, as a controlled testbed. Across both studied models, we identify a reproducible three-phase rebound pattern: models first attempt to rewrite the evaluator but fail, as their rewrites embed test cases their own solutions cannot pass. They then temporarily retreat to legitimate solving. When legitimate reward remains scarce, they rebound into successful hacking with qualitatively different strategies. Using representation engineering, we extract concept directions for shortcut, deception, and evaluation awareness from domain-general contrastive pairs and find that the shortcut direction tracks hacking behavior most closely, making it an effective representational proxy for detection. Motivated by this finding, we propose Advantage Modification, which integrates shortcut concept scores into GRPO advantage computation to penalize hacking rollouts before policy updates. Because the penalty is internalized into the training signal rather than applied only at inference time, Advantage Modification provides more robust suppression of hacking compared with generation-time activation steering.

</details>


### [34] [Soft MPCritic: Amortized Model Predictive Value Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01477)
*Thomas Banker,Nathan P. Lawrence,Ali Mesbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了soft MPCritic框架，通过在软价值空间中学习并结合基于采样的规划，实现了强化学习与模型预测控制的高效结合，解决了大规模计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习(RL)和模型预测控制(MPC)各有优势，但在大规模问题上将两者结合仍面临计算挑战，传统的直接长时域规划方法在复杂控制任务中可能失效。

Method: 提出soft MPCritic框架：1) 通过模型预测路径积分控制(MPPI)实例化MPC，用于在线控制和价值目标生成；2) 使用拟合价值迭代训练终端Q函数，使学习到的价值函数与规划器对齐；3) 引入分摊预热策略，回收规划的开环动作序列用于计算批量MPPI价值目标；4) 采用基于场景的规划方式，使用动态模型集成进行下一步预测。

Result: 在经典和复杂控制任务上验证了该方法能够通过鲁棒的短时域规划有效学习，同时保持了解决方案质量，并降低了计算成本，证明了该方法作为MPC策略综合实用且可扩展的蓝图的有效性。

Conclusion: soft MPCritic为在策略提取和直接长时域规划可能失效的场景下合成MPC策略提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案框架，成功实现了RL与MPC的优势互补。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) and model predictive control (MPC) offer complementary strengths, yet combining them at scale remains computationally challenging. We propose soft MPCritic, an RL-MPC framework that learns in (soft) value space while using sample-based planning for both online control and value target generation. soft MPCritic instantiates MPC through model predictive path integral control (MPPI) and trains a terminal Q-function with fitted value iteration, aligning the learned value function with the planner and implicitly extending the effective planning horizon. We introduce an amortized warm-start strategy that recycles planned open-loop action sequences from online observations when computing batched MPPI-based value targets. This makes soft MPCritic computationally practical, while preserving solution quality. soft MPCritic plans in a scenario-based fashion with an ensemble of dynamic models trained for next-step prediction accuracy. Together, these ingredients enable soft MPCritic to learn effectively through robust, short-horizon planning on classic and complex control tasks. These results establish soft MPCritic as a practical and scalable blueprint for synthesizing MPC policies in settings where policy extraction and direct, long-horizon planning may fail.

</details>


### [35] [DISCO-TAB: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Synthesis of Complex Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01481)
*Arshia Ilaty,Hossein Shirazi,Amir Rahmani,Hajar Homayouni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DISCO-TAB框架，通过强化学习优化多目标判别器指导微调LLM，生成高保真、保护隐私且具有临床实用性的合成电子病历表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建临床决策支持系统时面临高质量且保护隐私的生物医学数据稀缺的问题。现有的生成式大语言模型在生成EHR数据时，难以捕捉复杂的非线性依赖关系和处理严重的类别不平衡，导致生成的记录虽统计合理但在临床上无效。

Method: 引入DISCO-TAB框架，将微调后的LLM与通过强化学习优化的多目标判别器系统相结合。不同于依赖标量反馈的方法，该框架在token、句子、特征和行四个粒度上评估合成质量，并整合了自动约束发现和逆频率奖励塑形机制，以自主保留潜在的医学逻辑并解决少数类崩溃问题。

Result: 在心力衰竭、帕金森病等高维小样本医学数据集上验证，该方法相比GAN和扩散模型基线，下游临床分类器效用提升高达38.2%，统计保真度极高（JSD < 0.01），并对成员推理攻击具有鲁棒的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一种为敏感医疗应用生成可信赖、保留实用性的合成表格数据的新标准，证明了分层反馈机制在合成数据生成中的有效性。

Abstract: The development of robust clinical decision support systems is frequently impeded by the scarcity of high-fidelity, privacy-preserving biomedical data. While Generative Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for synthetic data generation, they often struggle to capture the complex, non-linear dependencies and severe class imbalances inherent in Electronic Health Records (EHR), leading to statistically plausible but clinically invalid records. To bridge this gap, we introduce DISCO-TAB (DIScriminator-guided COntrol for TABular synthesis), a novel framework that orchestrates a fine-tuned LLM with a multi-objective discriminator system optimized via Reinforcement Learning. Unlike prior methods relying on scalar feedback, DISCO-TAB evaluates synthesis at four granularities, token, sentence, feature, and row, while integrating Automated Constraint Discovery and Inverse-Frequency Reward Shaping to autonomously preserve latent medical logic and resolve minority-class collapse. We rigorously validate our framework across diverse benchmarks, including high-dimensional, small-sample medical datasets (e.g., Heart Failure, Parkinson's). Our results demonstrate that hierarchical feedback yields state-of-the-art performance, achieving up to 38.2% improvement in downstream clinical classifier utility compared to GAN and Diffusion baselines, while ensuring exceptional statistical fidelity (JSD < 0.01) and robust resistance to membership inference attacks. This work establishes a new standard for generating trustworthy, utility-preserving synthetic tabular data for sensitive healthcare applications.

</details>


### [36] [Matching Accuracy, Different Geometry: Evolution Strategies vs GRPO in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01499)
*William Hoy,Binxu Wang,Xu Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现，进化策略（ES）在性能上可媲美基于梯度的GRPO方法，但两者在参数空间中形成几何结构截然不同的解：ES更新幅度大且偏离显著，GRPO更新局部且精准，而这两个近乎正交的解却在损失景观中线性连通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略（ES）已作为一种可扩展的无梯度方法，用于大语言模型微调，但其与基于梯度的强化学习方法（如GRPO）在达到相似任务性能时，所得到的模型参数解是否也相似，尚不清楚。本文旨在系统对比这两种方法在参数空间中的行为差异与内在机制。

Method: 在四个任务上，分别在单任务学习和序列持续学习两种设置下，对比ES与群组相对策略优化（GRPO）的性能表现。通过分析模型参数更新的幅度、KL散度漂移以及更新方向的正交性，研究两种方法的几何差异。进一步，通过验证解之间的线性连通性（是否存在损失屏障），揭示其损失景观结构。最后，发展了一套ES的解析理论，统一解释上述现象。

Result: 在单任务设置中，ES的准确率匹配或超越GRPO；在持续学习设置中，当迭代预算受控时，ES仍具竞争力。然而，两种方法产生的模型更新截然不同：ES的参数变化幅度大，引起更广的非任务KL漂移；GRPO的更新则更小、更局部。惊人地，ES与GRPO的解在损失景观中线性连通且无损失屏障，尽管它们的更新方向近乎正交。解析理论表明，ES能在弱信息方向上积累大量非任务移动，同时在任务相关方向上取得足够进展，从而匹敌梯度方法。

Conclusion: 无梯度与基于梯度的微调方法可以到达性能相似但在几何上迥异的解。ES在弱信息方向上的大幅移动可能导致知识遗忘，而GRPO的局部更新更有利于知识保持。这一发现对模型持续学习、知识保留及模型微调策略的选择具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have emerged as a scalable gradient-free alternative to reinforcement learning based LLM fine-tuning, but it remains unclear whether comparable task performance implies comparable solutions in parameter space. We compare ES and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) across four tasks in both single-task and sequential continual-learning settings. ES matches or exceeds GRPO in single-task accuracy and remains competitive sequentially when its iteration budget is controlled. Despite this similarity in task performance, the two methods produce markedly different model updates: ES makes much larger changes and induces broader off-task KL drift, whereas GRPO makes smaller, more localized updates. Strikingly, the ES and GRPO solutions are linearly connected with no loss barrier, even though their update directions are nearly orthogonal. We develop an analytical theory of ES that explains all these phenomena within a unified framework, showing how ES can accumulate large off-task movement on weakly informative directions while still making enough progress on the task to match gradient-based RL in downstream accuracy. These results show that gradient-free and gradient-based fine-tuning can reach similarly accurate yet geometrically distinct solutions, with important consequences for forgetting and knowledge preservation. The source code is publicly available: https://github.com/Bhoy1/ESvsGRPO.

</details>


### [37] [Beyond Logit Adjustment: A Residual Decomposition Framework for Long-Tailed Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01506)
*Zhanliang Wang,Hongzhuo Chen,Quan Minh Nguyen,Mian Umair Ahsan,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了REPAIR方法，通过分解残差修正为类间项和成对项，解决了长尾分类中固定偏移量无法适应输入变化的问题，在多个基准测试上验证了成对修正的有效性和适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾分类中模型在推理时系统性地偏向频繁类。现有的后处理方法（如logit adjustment）通过添加固定的类别偏移量来修正，但这种固定偏移量无法适应不同输入间的变化，因为恢复两个类别相对排序所需的修正量可能因输入而异。

Method: 通过在基模型top-k短列表上进行贝叶斯最优重排序来研究问题。将最优分数与基线分数之间的差距（残差修正）分解为：1）类间分量（在每个类内恒定）；2）成对分量（依赖于输入和竞争标签）。基于此分解开发了REPAIR方法，这是一种轻量级的后处理重排序器，结合了收缩稳定的类间项和由短列表上竞争特征驱动的线性成对项。

Result: 理论分析表明：当残差纯为类间时，固定偏移足以恢复贝叶斯最优排序；当相同标签对在不同上下文中产生不兼容的排序约束时，固定偏移无法实现恢复。实验在五个基准测试（图像分类、物种识别、场景识别和罕见病诊断）上验证了该分解能够解释成对修正何时有帮助、何时仅类间修正就足够。

Conclusion: 本文通过残差分解理论揭示了固定偏移量方法的局限性，并提出了可测试的预测来判断成对修正何时能提升性能。REPAIR方法成功结合类间和成对修正，在多个领域验证了理论分析的正确性。

Abstract: Long-tailed classification, where a small number of frequent classes dominate many rare ones, remains challenging because models systematically favor frequent classes at inference time. Existing post-hoc methods such as logit adjustment address this by adding a fixed classwise offset to the base-model logits. However, the correction required to restore the relative ranking of two classes need not be constant across inputs, and a fixed offset cannot adapt to such variation. We study this problem through Bayes-optimal reranking on a base-model top-k shortlist. The gap between the optimal score and the base score, the residual correction, decomposes into a classwise component that is constant within each class, and a pairwise component that depends on the input and competing labels. When the residual is purely classwise, a fixed offset suffices to recover the Bayes-optimal ordering. We further show that when the same label pair induces incompatible ordering constraints across contexts, no fixed offset can achieve this recovery. This decomposition leads to testable predictions regarding when pairwise correction can improve performance and when cannot. We develop REPAIR (Reranking via Pairwise residual correction), a lightweight post-hoc reranker that combines a shrinkage-stabilized classwise term with a linear pairwise term driven by competition features on the shortlist. Experiments on five benchmarks spanning image classification, species recognition, scene recognition, and rare disease diagnosis confirm that the decomposition explains where pairwise correction helps and where classwise correction alone suffices.

</details>


### [38] [Learning ECG Image Representations via Dual Physiological-Aware Alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01526)
*Hung Manh Pham,Jialu Tang,Aaqib Saeed,Dong Ma,Bin Zhu,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ECG-Scan自监督框架，通过多模态对比对齐和软导联约束从心电图图像中学习临床通用表示，缩小了图像与信号分析间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球大量心电图数据仅以图像形式存在，而现有自动化分析方法依赖原始信号记录，限制了其在现实和资源受限场景中的应用。

Method: 提出ECG-Scan自监督框架：1）通过图像与金标准信号-文本模态之间的多模态对比对齐优化图像表示学习；2）引入软导联约束集成领域知识，规范重建过程并改善信号导联间的一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集和下游任务上的广泛基准测试表明，该基于图像的模型相比现有图像基线实现更优性能，显著缩小了心电图图像与信号分析之间的差距。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了自监督图像建模在释放大规模历史心电图数据潜力方面的价值，有望拓宽自动化心血管诊断的可及性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are among the most widely used diagnostic tools for cardiovascular diseases, and a large amount of ECG data worldwide appears only in image form.
